# AutoTune product roadmap and current status

Last reconciled: **2026-11-08**. Owner: the project owner; implementation agents maintain the evidence and session handoff below.

## Product goal — do not substitute a different product

Automate the tedious GPU tuning loop that currently requires manual increments and repeated benchmarks in MSI Afterburner:

1. Choose **Performance**, **Efficiency** or **Balanced** and a time budget.
2. Approve the testing session; AutoTune captures a repeatable baseline.
3. Try qualified settings, benchmark candidates, reject errors and stop safely.
4. Validate the best candidate more thoroughly and restore the starting settings.
5. Present a before/after comparison and an optional, separately consented profile application.

A valid outcome is **no better profile found** or **inconclusive**. A few benchmarks can find a promising configuration, not prove universal stability or a global optimum.

Initial target: Windows and the owner's **RTX 2070 SUPER**. Direct undervolting/V/F-curve editing is not established. Power-limit efficiency tuning must not be marketed as undervolting. CPU/BIOS/RAM writes and additional vendors are deferred.

Read-only discovery and benchmarking are foundations, **not fulfillment of the automatic-optimization goal**. A benchmark-first public MVP requires an explicit owner scope decision. If a required tuning gate proves infeasible, stop and discuss the scope; do not silently replace the goal or weaken safety requirements.

## How to read this plan

- This is the canonical **product-level progress and priority overview**, reconstructed from the owner's pasted feasibility session of 2026-10-14 and reconciled against the repository on 2026-10-24. The original research was delivered in chat, not as a checked-in PRD. This document preserves its E0–E8/story IDs and A–E release gates; it does not claim every research proposal was approved.
- **Done** means the stated story's exit criterion has supporting evidence. **Partial** means only a subset exists. **In progress** means active unfinished work. **Not started** means no implementation was identified in the inspected workspace. **Blocked** names an unmet prerequisite, not an estimate.
- Epic status is not the average of checked boxes. All required stories and applicable acceptance criteria must pass before an epic closes. A completed narrow implementation spec does not automatically complete its broader epic.
- This overview does not override detailed safety contracts or grant permission to execute workloads, touch hardware, elevate, commit or push. Existing approvals remain scoped to their respective work. Resolve contradictions explicitly with the owner.
- Evidence labels: **documented**, **simulated**, **Windows-observed**, **hardware-verified**, or **unresolved**. Fake success cannot qualify real tuning. Historical results remain dated; current verification is bound to exact revisions/manifests below.

## Where we are now

**2026-10-29 reconciliation:** the stop/completion-window
and aggregate metric-slot defects are now resolved, independently reviewed and
verified by 26 focused CPU-only envelope tests, Clippy and formatting on October 28.
The envelope and Windows clock adapter are committed; the selective NVML getter
seam is also committed. Earlier handoffs/checkpoints retain their dated blocked
or uncommitted states as history, not current instructions. This reconciliation
inspected Git and source/contracts; it did not rerun their tests or hosted CI.
The later October 29 sampler implementation milestone below adds integrated
source and CPU evidence; executed live integration remains unqualified.
E3 is partial and Gate B unmet.

**Current track: E3 measurement integration**, per the owner's priority, reaffirmed on 2026-10-29. E1.1 retains its documented deterministic foundation; hosted CI was explicitly retired by the owner on 2026-11-04. The synthetic measurement foundation, pure FMA reference, bounded worker, compile-only CUDA adapter, monitor and evidence envelope remain delivered. The independent sampler source now connects selective NVML reads, shared-clock/controller supervision, Windows containment/transport and bounded raw/artifact export, with independent static review and CPU verification. A separately scoped pilot workload declaration now binds the approved three-stage plans, device/artifacts and original run origins. Encoded worker handshake and child admission-state source are also delivered; encoded dispatch/failure-vector transport is now delivered as well. Parent dispatch-report assessment now checks call/clock/phase/count assertions, and actual CUDA adapter calls now have bounded raw tracing including upload completion. Bound producer source now composes prepared buffers, actual driver tracing, full checking/hash/reporting, stop-drain and first-failure export. A selected-current-settings collector now supplies bound raw fact artifacts, with a separate one-shot request/response exchange and strict parent receipt reader. A Windows pipe bridge now supplies owned producer/receipt handoffs and split control ownership, and a selected-settings dispatcher composes actual pipe polling/getters/response handoff in source. Fixed pilot process startup now reuses Windows containment with strict argument/image binding and retained failure owners. Common child startup/bootstrap composes actual endpoint/clock/image/pipe preparation and preserves parent setup deadlines; independent pilot control source now retains original stop windows, raw failures and separate explicit cleanup facts. Pilot-bound telemetry now composes the selected getters, raw clock/field ledger and strict receipt conversion with concrete pipe polling. A fixed sampler executable entry composes these paths with independently watched original setup, retained failed-session cleanup and bounded drain. Parent sampler scheduling/receipt source now composes untrusted report checks, monitor/re-entry policy, scoped original-epoch storage and reserved stop traffic. A distinct starting-setting scope/artifact now supplies pre-manifest collection/reading and reference/equality checks in source without a placeholder pilot declaration; a source-level one-shot NativeGrant sender now requires retained environment/starting settings, context, peers, consent, fresh process/safety/re-entry and watcher evidence, and child token construction binds the accepted grant to the exact setup artifact. Windows `ParentSamplerChannel` and `ParentWorkerChannel` are now joined by a source-level `ParentAdmission` coordinator that polls sampler/setup/control in bounded rounds, holds setup and controller owners, and sequences preflight, watch-gated Start and NativeGrant. Its retained owners now transfer the exact sampler/worker process pair into retirement assets and reassemble starting/setup/report evidence for the existing pilot finalizer, returning every owner on setup or binding failure. ParentAdmission now requires a `starting_launch::AdmissionProcess` produced only from an eligible `LaunchGuard`; the same process and launch facts must reach finalizer transfer, and launch facts enter the workload raw export. The admission poll path requests both sampler and worker process stop events and queues one sampler Stop frame on parent-lane failure, retaining request/send outcomes without claiming exit or GPU quiescence. A separate failure-finalizer path can collect host retirement after a retained parent fault without successful worker completion. Complete setup is used when available; if the setup receiver was never created, the finalizer can archive an explicit missing-setup marker. A partially received setup now retains and exports its raw packet owners and an explicit status artifact without implying Ready or preflight acceptance. Missing-controller and overflow/capacity cases can still prevent export and return owners. This remains typed source composition only: the fixed parent executable and full launch-to-retirement loop are still absent; actual Windows sampler/native qualification and useful repeated baselines remain incomplete. E5/E6 recovery remains partial, not the default expansion track. We are not yet implementing the optimizer.

Starting response v2 now includes retained NVML runtime/file/loader/init and file-clock facts with strict parent reading; legacy v1 remains explicit without runtime facts. The worker now reads its own fixed cache/EAGER environment before DLL loading and checks effective CUDA module loading before context/module setup; a bound worker setup artifact/strict structural reader now retains these facts and full raw histories before Ready, with one-shot setup chunks on the original output sequence and strict read-before-Ready reception retaining the decoder for later dispatch. ReadyDelivery now retains a reported setup consistency assessment and poisons its decoder on rejection; ParentSampler additionally retains one-shot host/starting/runtime/setup/current reference checks, with reported starting phase/cleanup/receipt timing now screened and local launcher/channel provenance compared against retained process owners; separate consent request/console receipt source now guards fixed launch and parent retains checked setup/workload decisions, while timestamped retained-handle process/containment collection now runs inside a one-shot parent Start intent with fresh safety/re-entry checks around collection, encoding and queueing. A unique original-handle StopOwner now separates control from collection and shares first pending/returned stop requests with Process; fixed parent controller/four independent request lanes now enforce original setup/run/safety/stop deadlines in source. The unique controller publisher is now attached directly to parent Start/receipt/tick/fault paths, with readiness required at every Start boundary and sticky publication failures. Normal host-controller retirement source now gates original process/job exit evidence and shares force arbitration across launch/process/control ownership. A fixed asynchronous retirement collector now retains the parent, controller, both process owners, clock and partial frame while one final original-handle observation runs off the deadline path; cancellation remains independent and collection success is not retirement acknowledgment. Complete session orchestration, remaining query/phase/native-call supervision, native authority transfer and observed qualification remain open. A [pre-manifest host artifact](docs/benchmark-worker.md#pre-manifest-host-environment--2026-11-05) now binds reported OS/architecture/elevation/power facts and separately sourced owner assertions, with composed starting/environment checks retained by the parent. The starting launch/bootstrap handoff is delivered in source. ### E7 — Conditional optimizer · Not started; blocked on E3/E4/E6

Dependencies: E3, E4, E6. Exit: bounded automatic exploration produces an independently validated recommendation or an honest original/no-improvement outcome.

| Story | Status | Exit criterion |
| --- | --- | --- |
| E7.1 Single-parameter search | Not started | Versioned deterministic policy, qualified bounds, time budget and safety stops; start with one parameter, not unrestricted multi-control search. |
| E7.2 Ranking and stopping rules | Not started | Define/review versioned objective metrics and budgets; repeat promising candidates, distinguish improvement/regression/inconclusive, retain raw evidence and terminate within policy. |
| E7.3 Independent final validation | Not started | Validate finalist beyond ranking tests, restore original settings before recommendation and allow no better candidate. Profile application remains separate consent. |

Objective-policy decision owned by E7.2, with measurement requirements fed into E3.3: the original research **proposed** maximizing measured performance, minimizing GPU energy per unit of work subject to a performance-loss budget, and a Balanced performance/efficiency trade-off. Define units, workload aggregation, uncertainty handling, loss budgets, thresholds and Balanced selection rules before claiming those modes implemented. These policies/values remain unapproved; missing power/energy evidence cannot support an efficiency claim.

## Release gates — original A–E, not G1–G7

| Gate | Current state | Required evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A — Coherent API/license/safety contract | Partial | Close/review E0 decisions; resolve relevant contract inconsistencies and licensing questions. |
| B — Useful repeatable read-only baseline | Not met | E2–E4 baseline/correctness evidence, not just successful telemetry reads. |
| C — One recoverable real control | Blocked | Accepted applicable recovery prerequisites and E6.2 hardware evidence. Fake tests or a successful setter alone do not pass. |
| D — Complete tuning workflow | Not met | E7 plus relevant E8 workflow validated on an explicitly qualified configuration. |
| E — Release review | Not met | Safety, packaging, licensing and documentation reviewed for the declared support envelope. |

The recovery architecture has separate **W01–W24** acceptance cases and **G1–G7** prerequisites: guardian independence; protected ownership; storage consistency; native quiescence; physical-entry timing; trusted consent/isolation; and hardware recovery. Its **P0–P3** labels are proof tiers, **not** the original product-priority labels. Selected P1 Windows evidence does not prove P2 OS-crash persistence or P3 hardware qualification. None of G1–G7 is declared fully closed here; especially G4/G5 cannot be solved merely by adding more fake tests.

## Next work and decision checkpoints

1. **Owner-prioritized track — E3 integration after E1.1:** Existing synthetic mechanisms/6A, Q1–Q4, envelope fixes, the pure bridge and independent sampler implementation are settled software slices; do not restart them. Next prepare source/artifact-bound Windows fixture execution evidence and a separate exact read-only capture manifest, while continuing the worker/device/manifest/consent integration source. Publication is authorized at completed milestones. Process/native/GPU execution remains separately consented; software/source publication does not qualify it. Before baseline assessment, implement the approved thermal-quarter/boundary attribution and repeatability contract. No winner or recovery expansion.
2. **Before expanding recovery again:** report which remaining gates are software tasks, host-dependent tests or unresolved native feasibility constraints. Ask the owner about any unavailable prerequisite rather than inventing a stronger guarantee or endlessly widening implementation.
3. **Next product-facing track — E3.1–E3.3:** build on the synthetic recorder toward an approved workload's bounded lifecycle and telemetry integration; define cadence/freshness/gap and repeat-baseline acceptance without inventing numeric defaults. This may proceed independently of real writes. It does not authorize benchmark execution now or replace the tuning goal with a read-only release.
4. **Before any real write:** explicitly assess Gate C feasibility, applicable G1–G7 and the separate consent/privilege/recovery decisions. Passing fake infrastructure alone never authorizes E6.2.

These are documentation/priority checkpoints, not new implementation or hardware authorizations. No calendar promises are made before feasibility is settled.

### Delivery packages — owner-selected order, 2026-10-29

These packages group the existing story IDs; they are not replacement epics or a
second status ledger. Work through one bounded reviewed/tested slice at a time.
Software implementation may proceed under the selected direction; native loads,
workloads, hardware writes, installation/elevation and publication retain their
separate authorization boundaries. Cross-cutting E0 decisions are resolved when
needed, not deferred past the dependent work.

| Package / stories | Deliverable and exit | Dependencies / decision boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 — Current-state reconciliation | One current overview with historical evidence clearly separated, exact next integration gap and this dependency order. | Documentation/source/Git inspection only; not a new test acceptance. |
| 2 — First real measurement chain / E2.1, E3.1–E3.2, E4.1 | Independently supervised sampler and worker; shared-clock timestamps; exact GPU/artifact/manifest binding; bounded IPC/evidence; complete reference checking and observed exits. A separately consented pilot either produces attributable evidence or an explicit stopped/invalid/inconclusive result. | First raw-field/monitor mapping, then sampler transport/containment, then controller/worker/consent/live-producer integration. Keep native admission unavailable until prerequisites hold. Pilot execution needs a fresh exact manifest. E0 API/license/source obligations relevant to native loading must be resolved before it. |
| 3 — Useful repeated baseline / E3.3 and remaining Gate B evidence | Frozen required facts, exact measurement/thermal-quarter attribution, complete independent repeats, variation/drift and coverage assessment; an honest usable or invalid/inconclusive outcome. Explicitly audit remaining E2–E4 acceptance; one narrow FMA baseline does not close all E4 or Gate B. | Package 2 evidence; approved profile remains unchanged. Pilot and acceptance data stay separate. No live energy/efficiency claim without a separately approved estimator and source coverage. |
| 4 — Recoverable real control / E0, E5, E6.1–E6.2 | Classify remaining W/G work into software, host tests and unresolved native feasibility; complete applicable recovery acceptance and qualify one useful control's write/readback/restoration. | Gate A contracts and applicable G1–G7; exact hardware/privilege/consent manifest. Missing native feasibility is a decision checkpoint, not permission for endless fake-test expansion. Do not postpone this feasibility assessment until an optimizer is built. |
| 5 — Bounded optimizer / E7.1–E7.3 | Versioned objective/ranking policy, one-parameter bounded search, independent finalist validation and restoration before recommendation; allow original/no-improvement/inconclusive results. | E3/E4/E6 acceptance. Performance/Efficiency/Balanced definitions and evidence requirements need explicit approval; approve required live-energy work before claiming efficiency. Extra controls/E6.3 require separate qualification. |
| 6 — Integrated product and release / E8, remaining E0–E4 | Discovery/consent/stop/recovery workflow, reports/history/comparison, separately consented profile application, packaging/license/safety review and Gate A–E acceptance audit. | UI planning can precede hardware qualification, but cannot bypass it. Before release, explicitly disposition E4.2 VRAM, E4.3 error observation and every other required open story; this package cannot silently drop them or redefine a benchmark-only MVP. |

Completed first implementation subtask: [raw-field/monitor bridge plan and execution record](docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-29-e3-sampler-bridge.md).
The [independent sampler implementation record](docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-29-e3-independent-sampler.md)
closes the next software subtask. Package 2 still needs its process/native
acceptance and worker/device/consent chain; neither implementation milestone
implies full measurement-chain acceptance.

## Session protocol — keep the roadmap current

**At the start of each implementation/planning session:**

1. Read this file and its latest handoff before selecting work; inspect the working tree without discarding changes.
2. Identify the exact epic/story and bounded exit criterion. Read its active implementation spec, safety contracts and latest evidence. Do not infer completion from artifact presence or old headings. If a required local contract is absent, stop the affected implementation/acceptance work and ask the owner to supply/recover it; do not reconstruct safety authority from this summary. Unrelated documentation can still proceed with the missing evidence marked unresolved.
3. If the user requests different work, record its epic mapping/priority decision. Ask before changing the product goal, safety contract or public-MVP scope.

**At the end of a session that changes implementation, evidence or priorities:**

1. Update affected story/epic/gate states here and the mutable task/evidence/change-log sections of the detailed implementation spec. Never silently edit frozen approved intent or acceptance contracts; changed scope/contract requires explicit owner renegotiation. Include dated source/test evidence, failures, ignored/blocked cases and what was not run. Bind new test claims to the full baseline commit plus relevant working-tree/source/artifact hashes or a retained evidence manifest; otherwise state that results are not verified against the current tree. Do not rewrite past failures into passes.
2. Replace the current handoff below with: work completed, remaining blockers, next bounded action and required owner decisions. Append a short dated checkpoint entry.
3. Mark `Last reconciled` accurately. If evidence conflicts or cannot be verified, keep the item partial/unresolved and explain it; do not create a competing progress file.
4. Report progress toward the product goal in the user handoff, not only test counts. Completion of documentation or a local spec does not mean product/release acceptance.

### Previous parent starting-handoff handoff — 2026-11-04

- **Owner decision — hosted CI retired:** all GitHub Actions workflows are removed at the owner's explicit request on 2026-11-04. No push, PR or manual hosted trigger remains. Continue appropriate local checks and milestone pushes; do not recreate Actions or expect a hosted run for subsequent pushes. Earlier failures and passes stay historical. The strict starting-response reader is currently implemented/tested in the working tree; its final verification and publication remain separate from this CI-policy change.
- **Latest progress — E3 parent-side starting launch handoff:** `workload::starting_launch::Handoff` (Windows only) composes the parent-side launch boundary: it builds the `Session`, freezes the encoded starting `Frame`, then acquires the one-shot starting sender and sends exactly that frame. `send` makes at most one attempt, re-stores the exact unsent frame and the first `SendFailure`, and never re-acquires a sender; `poll` delegates to `Session::poll_process`, so the arrival deadline, original-epoch conversion and sticky faults remain the session's. `Session::observe` now exposes that epoch conversion portably (a mismatched token, regression or missing observation yields `None`, never a restarted base), which also removes the cross-target dead-code warnings without a target-specific allow. The launched process stays caller-owned: no process is created here, no sender touches a real pipe and no workload `NativeAdmission` or qualification is issued.
- **Verification:** baseline `c17ac64` plus unchanged before/after source inventories binds **483 CPU-only tests** (402 integration across 40 test binaries, including 21 reception/session/handoff cases; 32 probe ABI fakes, 24 worker feature-library fakes/static, 25 reference/prepared), scoped all-feature warning-denied Clippy, formatting and full-workspace/all-target/all-feature compile/link. Manifest `target/workload-verification/starting-handoff2-manifest.json`, SHA-256 `add9dab0777591e4dcc149343514ca153a1118dcabc12abc68c28eb3c5b0321e`. The initial slice stays recorded as `starting-handoff1` (482 tests). ### Previous starting reception handoff — 2026-11-03

## Evidence map and BMAD navigation

Checked-in/source-level entry points: [documentation index](docs/README.md), [probe](crates/autotune-probe), [simulator](crates/autotune-power-trial), [recovery evidence](docs/windows-recovery.md), [shared Windows contract](docs/windows-recovery-contract.md), [storage contract](docs/windows-storage-v1.md).

**Local BMAD artifacts** below exist in this workspace but `_bmad-output/` is git-ignored. They may be absent from a fresh clone. Keep the product overview here version-control eligible; do not assume local evidence is shared or silently change ignore rules. Historical entries in those files may describe an older implementation state; use the dated evidence and current task list together.

- Completed implementation specs: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-read-only-nvidia-feasibility-probe.md`, `spec-modern-nvml-clock-offset-reads.md`, `spec-mock-guarded-power-limit-trial.md` (all in that directory).
- Active implementation spec: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-windows-fake-worker-recovery.md`.
- Completed E1/E3 implementation specs: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-e1-1-workspace-ci.md`, `spec-e3-2-synthetic-telemetry.md`, `spec-e3-3-synthetic-energy.md` (same directory). Only E1.1 has closed its full documented roadmap story; E3 specs close narrow synthetic subtasks.
- Repeat-run retention implementation record: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-e3-3-repeat-evidence.md`; only a synthetic E3.3 subtask, not baseline acceptance.
- Synthetic lifecycle implementation record: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-e3-1-synthetic-lifecycle.md`; owner decisions and internal model only, not a licensed/executing benchmark backend.
- Synthetic warm-up summary implementation record: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-e3-3-warmup-summary.md`; descriptive observation summaries only, not statistical sufficiency or completed baselines.
- Selected metadata implementation record: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-e3-3-metadata-differences.md`; exact selected caller assertions/unknowns, not run comparability or baseline acceptance.
- Pure FMA contract implementation record: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-e3-fma-pure-contract.md`; local/offline design/input/reference delivery, not native correctness/baseline qualification; shared contract in `docs/fma-reference.md`.
- Bounded worker implementation record: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-e3-windows-benchmark-worker.md`; local deterministic/owned-Windows-fake verification and compile-only CUDA/PTX, not live admission or hardware qualification; shared contract in `docs/benchmark-worker.md`.
- Injected monitor implementation record: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-e3-injected-monitor-policy.md`; injected clock/schedule/admission policy only, not live sampling or baseline qualification; shared contract in `docs/benchmark-worker.md`.
- Phase-1 live-evidence codec implementation record: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-e3-live-evidence-primitives.md`; canonical bytes/digests/strict decoding only, no session envelope, live producer or qualification authority; shared contract in `docs/live-evidence.md`.
- Phase-2 session-envelope implementation record: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-e3-live-evidence-envelope.md`; typed fields/digest/cross-field validation only, no live producer, execution, consent or qualification authority; shared contract in `docs/live-evidence-envelope.md`.
- First real benchmark planning spec: `_bmad-output/specs/spec-e3-first-real-benchmark/SPEC.md`, with `decision-brief.md`, `integration-contract.md`, `baseline-policy.md`, `verification-plan.md`, `qualification-profile.md`, `cuda-abi-contract.md`, `live-evidence-schema.md`, `monitor-integration-scope.md`, `live-evidence-codec-scope.md` and `live-evidence-envelope-scope.md`; Q1–Q4 accepted including complete numeric profile design, proposed ABI/live-schema seams, no implementation/execution approval.
